Reference classification was performed manually, obtaining approximately 24 million classified pix-els for the first area and approximately 3.8 million for the second. This research study included an analysis of the impact of the season on the threshold values for the tested VIs and the impact of image patch size provided as inputs for the NNs on classification accuracy. The results of the con-ducted research study indicate a higher classification accuracy using NNs (about 96%) compared with the best of the tested VIs, i.e., Excess Blue (about 87%). Due to the highly imbalanced nature of the used datasets (non-urbanized areas constitute approximately 87% of the total datasets), the Mat-thews correlation coefficient was also used to assess the correctness of the classification. The analysis based on statistical measures was supplemented with a qualitative assessment of the classification results, which allowed the identification of the most important sources of differences in classification between VIs thresholding and NNs.

</details>


### [175] [Learning Skills from Historical Action Trajectories: Action Experience Dictionary for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40219)
*Qi Lyu,Jiahua Dong,Hao Shen,Xudong Wang,Hongyuan Yu,Baichen Liu,Henghui Ding,Zhi Han,Nicu Sebe,Ivan Laptev,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Action Experience Dictionary (AED)，将历史动作轨迹编码为共享动作嵌入，支持跨任务技能复用与语义关系建模，并引入运动感知转移损失以减少背景干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models 虽结合视觉动态预测与动作生成，但无法显式复用跨操作任务的动作经验；同时冗余背景元素干扰关键视觉信息提取，难以捕捉指导目标动作预测的跨任务语义关系。

Method: 1) 聚合历史动作并与视觉观测对齐，用预训练动作分词器从 AED 中检索动作嵌入；2) 通过交叉注意力对池化嵌入进行视觉条件化，并将其前置到含噪动作 token，为预测提供交互上下文与动作意图；3) 提出运动感知转移损失，在随机时间间隔上监督视觉特征变化预测，建模动作相关运动并降低对无关背景线索的依赖。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实世界跨具身（cross-embodiment）设置上的实验验证了 AED 的有效性。

Conclusion: AED 通过共享动作嵌入字典实现动作经验复用与跨任务语义建模，配合运动感知转移损失，有效提升 World Action Models 的动作预测能力，并在仿真与真实跨具身场景中取得验证。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple visual dynamics prediction with action generation, yet they do not explicitly support the reuse of action experience across manipulation tasks. Furthermore, existing WAMs struggle to capture underlying cross-task semantic relationships that could guide target action prediction, as redundant background elements interfere with the extraction of key visual information. To address these challenges, we develop a novel Action Experience Dictionary (AED) that encodes historical physical action trajectories into shared action embeddings to support skill reuse and model cross-task relationships. Specifically, we first aggregate historical actions to align with visual observations and retrieve action embeddings from the AED using a pretrained action tokenizer. Subsequently, we visually condition the pooled embeddings through cross-attention and prepend them to noisy action tokens, providing interaction context and action intent for prediction. To model action-related motion and reduce reliance on irrelevant background cues, we introduce a motion-aware transition loss that supervises visual feature change prediction over random temporal intervals. Experiments on simulation benchmarks and in real-world cross-embodiment settings verify the effectiveness of our AED. The anonymous project website is available at \href{https://github.com/JiahuaDong/AED}{AED}.

</details>


### [176] [LOCI: Spatial Linear Memory for Streaming World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40222)
*Ji Xia,Tingting Liao,Xuezhi Liang,Hao Li,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOCI提出混合空间记忆架构，结合键值缓存与循环线性注意力记忆，并利用相机几何条件化读写，在重访场景时更忠实重建内容且显著降低内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型在相机重访已观测区域时应重现此前内容，需同时记住过去观测并针对当前视角检索正确信息。键值缓存保留视觉细节但随视频长度增长，循环记忆虽紧凑却将历史压缩为固定状态，导致单条历史观测无法直接访问。

Method: 在同一模型中混合两种记忆：一半transformer块的主注意力保留过去观测的键值缓存，另一半限制在当前块并由循环线性注意力记忆补充；该记忆的读写以投影相机几何为条件，使视角同时影响记忆寻址与存储内容；循环读出流入后续缓存块，为其查询提供累积场景上下文。

Result: 在公开MIND记忆基准及留出录制轨迹上，LOCI比代表性世界模型和同配方全softmax模型更忠实地重现重访内容；在完整历史下，等长度时峰值内存比全softmax降低约30%；在有界保留观测库下，以恒定内存流式处理长视频，并在相同预算下仍比全softmax更忠实。

Conclusion: 几何条件化的缓存—循环混合记忆可在保持高保真重访重建的同时降低内存，并支持恒定内存的长视频流式处理。

Abstract: When a camera revisits a previously observed region, a video world model should reproduce what was there before. This requires both remembering past observations and retrieving the right one for the current viewpoint. Key-value caches preserve visual detail but grow with video length; recurrent memory is compact but compresses history into a fixed-size state, so individual past observations are no longer directly accessible. We introduce LOCI, a hybrid spatial-memory architecture that keeps both representations. In half of the transformer blocks, main attention keeps a key-value cache of past observations; in the other half, it is restricted to the current chunk and complemented by a recurrent linear-attention memory whose reads and writes are conditioned on projective camera geometry, so viewpoint enters both memory addressing and stored content. Recurrent readouts flow into subsequent cache-backed blocks and supply their queries with accumulated scene context. On the public MIND memory benchmark and on held-out recorded trajectories, LOCI reproduces revisited content more faithfully than representative world models and a same-recipe full-softmax model; with full history, it lowers peak memory at equal length by about 30% relative to full softmax. With a bounded bank of retained observations, it streams long videos at constant memory and remains more faithful than full softmax under the same budget.

</details>


### [177] [EviRover: Reinforcing Agentic Perception Beyond a Glance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40230)
*Kaixuan Fan,Kaituo Feng,Tianshuo Peng,Yilei Jiang,Manyuan Zhang,Junke Wang,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"证据不足下的感知"问题，将感知建模为可主动获取信息的智能体过程，并构建数据、基准与EviRover模型，显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉感知被设定为对图像的单次预测，假设图像内容与模型参数知识足以解答查询。但在真实场景中，细粒度视觉细节或知识密集、需实时更新的信息常导致该假设失效，即"证据不足下的感知"。

Method: 设计两套数据生成流程，产出EviRover-SFT-5K与EviRover-RL-12K用于训练；构建人工校验基准EviLens（688个实例、五类感知任务）；提出EviRover，首个显式训练以通过交互解决感知查询的感知智能体，采用监督微调后接智能体强化学习。

Result: 4B的EviRover在EviLens上平均超越其骨干模型30分，达到与先进专有模型相当的水平；增益可迁移至WebEyes、传统感知基准与通用多模态基准，在BrowseComp-VL上提升15分。

Conclusion: 将感知重构为可主动获取证据的智能体过程是有效方向，可通过专用数据与训练流程实现，且泛化性好；代码、模型与数据均已开源。

Abstract: Visual perception is conventionally formulated as a one-shot prediction from a single glance at the image, under the assumption that the image content and the model's parametric knowledge suffice to resolve the query. This assumption often fails in real-world scenarios that hinge on fine-grained visual details or require knowledge-intensive and up-to-date information. We term such cases \textit{perception under insufficient evidence} and formulate perception as an agentic process that can obtain information beyond a single glance. To address the absence of data for this setting, we design two dedicated data generation pipelines, yielding EviRover-SFT-5K and EviRover-RL-12K for training. We further construct EviLens, a human-verified benchmark comprising 688 instances across five perception categories. Building on these data, we present EviRover, to our knowledge the first perception agent explicitly trained to resolve perceptual queries through interaction, using supervised fine-tuning followed by agentic reinforcement learning. Experiments show that the 4B EviRover outperforms its backbone by 30 points on average on EviLens, reaching performance comparable to advanced proprietary models. The gains transfer beyond EviLens to WebEyes, conventional perception benchmarks, and general multimodal benchmarks, including a 15-point improvement on BrowseComp-VL. All code, models, and data are released.

</details>


### [178] [ComputerSD: Online Self-Distillation from Real-Time Feedback for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40253)
*Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Yizhou Liu,Bofan Chen,Tao Jiang,Wenhao Xu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ComputerSD 在线自蒸馏方法，将 GUI 转换的实时反馈转化为策略学习指导，联合优化 token 级自蒸馏与轨迹级 GRPO，在 OSWorld-Verified 上优于纯结果奖励 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体（CUA）在线训练主要依赖稀疏的结果奖励，无法为中间动作提供监督。而 on-policy 自蒸馏（OPSD）虽能提供 token 级信号，但直接应用于 CUA 在线训练存在两个问题：固定的指导可能与学生当前状态不匹配，以及指导引起的概率偏移可能与步骤级正确性冲突。

Method: ComputerSD 利用 GUI 转换的实时反馈生成指导。经微调的 GUI 分析器在每次动作后产出一份指导和一个步骤级价值分数：指导提供特权上下文，分数用于调节 OPSD 信号。方法在完全异步训练框架中联合优化 token 级 OPSD 与轨迹级 GRPO。

Result: 在 OSWorld-Verified 上，ComputerSD 相比仅用结果奖励的 GRPO，在通用的 Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking 和专用的 EvoCUA-8B 骨干上分别提升 1.9 与 4.1 个百分点；在分布外（OOD）设置中的评估进一步支持其泛化性。

Conclusion: 结果证明，通过在线自蒸馏从实时反馈中学习，对计算机使用智能体是有效的。

Abstract: Online training enables computer-use agents (CUAs) to improve through interaction with executable environments. However, existing methods primarily rely on sparse outcome rewards, which provide no supervision for intermediate actions. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers token-level learning signals through privileged rescoring, but directly applying it to CUA online training presents two challenges: fixed guidance may become misaligned with the student's current state, and guidance-induced probability shifts may conflict with step-level correctness. We introduce ComputerSD, an online self-distillation method for CUAs that converts real-time feedback from executed GUI transitions into guidance for policy learning. A fine-tuned GUI analyzer produces guidance and a step-level value score after each action; the guidance provides privileged context, while the score regulates the resulting OPSD signals. ComputerSD jointly optimizes token-level OPSD and trajectory-level GRPO in a fully asynchronous training framework. On OSWorld-Verified, ComputerSD outperforms outcome-only GRPO by 1.9 and 4.1 percentage points on the general-purpose Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking and specialized EvoCUA-8B backbones, respectively. Evaluation in out-of-distribution settings further supports the generalizability of ComputerSD. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of learning from real-time feedback through online self-distillation for CUAs.

</details>


### [179] [Looped Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40305)
*Yong Xien Chng,Tianyi Chen,Wenwen Tong,Haiwen Diao,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Looped-DiT，通过在去噪步骤内重复运行共享 Transformer 块增加计算深度，结合深度监督与自调制注意力，以更少参数和算力超越更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提升文生图模型依赖增大模型规模或增加去噪步数，本文探索在参数固定下通过循环计算增加深度来扩展计算的替代路径。

Method: 在每步去噪中重复运行共享 Transformer 块，并针对朴素循环的弱点（中间循环监督弱、注意力更新无约束侵蚀局部信息）提出跨循环深度监督与自调制注意力。

Result: 在匹配参数与匹配算力下持续优于非循环基线；260M 循环模型可超越 6.5 倍大模型，推理算力降低 4.9 倍；固定预算下增加循环深度比增加去噪步收益更大；深层循环能纠正早期错误，呈现潜在推理行为。

Conclusion: 循环计算为视觉生成模型扩展提供有前景的方向。

Abstract: Improving text-to-image models has traditionally relied on increasing model size or the number of denoising steps. In this work, we explore an alternative way to scale computation by repeatedly running shared Transformer blocks within each denoising step, effectively increasing computational depth while keeping the parameter count fixed. This looped computation enables iterative refinement of internal representations without explicit reasoning tokens. However, naive looping fails to consistently improve image quality. We trace this problem to weak supervision across intermediate loops and unregulated attention updates that progressively erode local information. To overcome these challenges, we propose Looped Diffusion Transformer (Looped-DiT), which combines deep supervision across intermediate loops with self-modulating attention to stabilize looped feature updates. Under matched-parameter and matched-compute settings, Looped-DiT consistently outperforms non-looped baselines. Notably, a 260M-parameter looped model can surpass a model 6.5x larger across multiple text-to-image benchmarks while requiring 4.9x lower inference compute. Beyond this performance gain, we find that looped computation can offer a more effective form of iterative computation for diffusion models, with increasing loop depth yielding larger gains than adding more denoising steps under a fixed inference budget. Furthermore, deeper loops can progressively correct mistakes made in earlier loops, exhibiting behaviors suggestive of latent reasoning. Together, these results show that looped computation offers a promising way to scale visual generation models.

</details>


### [180] [GLARE: Generating Listening Heads with Appropriate Reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40317)
*Zikai Liao,Yumin Suh,Yi Ouyang,Yi-Lun Lee,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Zhaozheng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对"倾听者头部生成"这一被忽视的问题，提出数据、模型、评价三位一体的解决方案：构建带细粒度反应标注的倾听数据集、提出音频驱动的 GLARE 基线模型，并设计以反应为中心的评价协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话头部生成研究多聚焦说话者，倾听行为（何时反应、如何反应、反应类型）研究不足。已有双人数据集缺乏细粒度倾听反应标注，且沿用说话头与视频生成的评价指标只衡量视觉真实感，无法判断倾听者反应是否恰当。

Method: 从 RealTalk 与 Seamless Interaction 构建倾听专用数据集，约 147 小时成对说话者-倾听者视频，含 64,557 条事件级反应标注，覆盖点头、摇头、微笑、大笑、皱眉、惊讶六类。提出基于流匹配 Transformer 的音频驱动基线 GLARE，使用 Qwen2-Audio 提取韵律条件，并引入时间反应损失显式监督逐帧反应。同时提出反应导向评价协议：反应发生（R-F1）、时间对齐（R-tIoU）、非对称时间偏差（R-ATD）与反应区域视觉质量（R-FID）。

Result: 实验表明，相较此前的倾听头部方法，在视觉保真度与反应级指标上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 反应感知的数据、建模与评价对于生成自然的倾听行为至关重要。

Abstract: While talking head generation has advanced rapidly, generating natural listener behavior in dyadic conversations, which know when to react, how to react, and with what type of response, remains underexplored. Existing dyadic datasets lack fine-grained listener reaction annotations, and prevailing evaluation metrics inherited from talking-head and video generation measure visual realism rather than whether a listener reacted appropriately. We address these gaps along three aspects. First, we curate a listening-head-specific dataset built from RealTalk and Seamless Interaction, comprising approximately 147 hours of paired speaker-listener videos with 64,557 event-level reaction annotations across six categories: nodding, head shaking, smiling, laughing, frowning, and surprised. Second, we introduce an audio-driven baseline built on a flow-matching transformer, namely GLARE, with prosody conditioning derived from Qwen2-Audio and a temporal reaction loss that explicitly supervises frame-wise reactions. Third, we propose a reaction-oriented evaluation protocol that jointly measures reaction occurrence (R-F1), temporal alignment (R-tIoU), asymmetric temporal deviation (R-ATD), and reaction-region visual quality (R-FID), giving a more behaviorally grounded assessment than visual-quality-only metrics. Experiment results show consistent gains over prior listening-head methods in both visual fidelity and reaction-level metrics, suggesting that reaction-aware data, modeling, and evaluation are critical for natural listening behavior.

</details>


### [181] [Atomizer-IO: Beyond Pixels, Patches and Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40320)
*Hugo Riffaud de Turckheim,Sylvain Lobry,Nicolas Houdré,Damien Robert,Roberto Interdonato,Diego Marcos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Atomizer-IO 架构，将观测数据作为原子表示，摆脱传统网格假设，通过局部交叉注意力映射到任意锚点，在遥感与三维点云任务中验证其灵活性与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数视觉架构假设观测位于规则网格上，这一抽象对自然图像有效，但面对通道、时间采样、空间分辨率和几何结构均可变的传感数据时过于受限。基于集合的通用架构虽去除了网格，却也舍弃了有用的空间归纳偏置。因此需要在保留空间归纳能力的同时摆脱网格约束。

Method: 提出 Atomizer-IO，采取"观测优先"的设计思路：基于数据的原子表示，每个观测由测量值和采集元数据共同描述；利用局部交叉注意力将观测映射到可任意放置的锚点上，从而从物理关系中推导结构，而非预设网格。

Result: 通过逐步放宽网格假设进行评测——从变化的输入栅格配置、不完整通道集，到灵活的输出密度，再到完全无栅格输入。Atomizer-IO 在多数任务上与灵活的地球观测专用架构具有竞争力，同时支持训练后对推理成本的控制，并具备有竞争力的计算-性能权衡。该公式还可无需架构重设计地扩展到无序三维点云。

Conclusion: 像素、图像块和网格不必定义传感架构的接口，原子化接口能超越规则栅格输入并实现广泛泛化。

Abstract: Most vision architectures assume that observations lie on a regular grid, an effective abstraction for natural images but a restrictive one for sensing data whose channels, temporal sampling, spatial resolution, and geometry can vary. Generic set-based architectures remove the grid, but also remove useful spatial inductive biases. We introduce Atomizer-IO, an architecture that places observations first and derives structure from their physical relationships. Building on top of an atomic representation of the data, each observation is described by its measurement and acquisition metadata, while local cross-attention maps observations to anchor points that can be arbitrarily placed. We evaluate this design by progressively relaxing the grid assumption, from varying input raster configurations and incomplete channel sets to flexible output density and, ultimately, inputs without a raster grid. Atomizer-IO is competitive with flexible EO-specific architectures on most tasks, while offering post-training control over inference cost and competitive compute--performance trade-offs. The same formulation extends without architectural redesign to unordered 3D point clouds, showing that the atomic interface generalizes beyond regular raster inputs. These results suggest that pixels, patches, and grids do not need to define the interface of a sensing architecture.

</details>


### [182] [MatLoom: Layered Text-to-Material Generation in a Compact Program Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40322)
*Anson Y. Lam,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出MatLoom，一种面向文本到材质生成的紧凑层导向程序语言，借助预训练语言模型生成可执行程序。程序通过共享空间表达组合带alpha遮罩的图层，显式定义覆盖度与PBR通道，并由独立解释器转为材质贴图，同时保留命名字段供后续编辑。无需任务特定微调，采用解析引导修复与预览批判修订设计，并进行噪声种子搜索。在141条提示、6个骨干模型上，最优配置在四项平面布局提示对齐指标上均超过三个扩散基线；盲测中渲染结果获59.2%选择率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材质生成不仅应产生外观，还应产生构造它的规则。现有方法侧重最终外观，缺乏对材质构造过程的可读、可编辑表达。

Method: 提出MatLoom：紧凑、层导向的文本到材质程序语言。每个程序组合带alpha遮罩的图层，共享空间表达式定义覆盖与PBR通道，显式表达图案、颜色和浮雕依赖。独立解释器将程序求值为材质贴图，源程序保留命名字段与层参数。流程无需任务特定微调，使用解析器引导修复和基于预览的批判来修订设计，然后搜索噪声种子并固定其余源。

Result: 在141条提示的基准上，用六个骨干模型评估；最优配置在四项平面布局提示对齐指标上的平均分均高于三个扩散基线。初始程序在平均BLIPScore上已超过三个基线（在批判或种子搜索之前）。保留程序跨骨干合并的中位长度为21行。30名参与者、20条提示的盲测四选一中，渲染结果获得59.2%选择，最受偏好基线为19.3%。

Conclusion: 紧凑的可执行程序为生成与提示对齐的材质提供了一种途径，同时将材质的构造过程保留为资产的一部分。

Abstract: Material generation should produce not only an appearance, but also the rules that construct it. We introduce MatLoom, a compact, layer-oriented language for text-to-material generation with pretrained language models. Each program composes alpha-masked layers whose shared spatial expressions define coverage and physically based rendering (PBR) channels, making dependencies between patterns, color, and relief explicit. A standalone interpreter evaluates the program into material maps, while the source retains named fields and layer parameters for subsequent authoring. Without task-specific fine-tuning, our pipeline uses parser-guided repair and preview-based critique to revise material designs, then searches noise seeds while keeping each candidate's remaining source fixed. On a curated benchmark of 141 prompts evaluated with six backbones, our best-performing configuration achieves higher mean scores than three diffusion baselines on all four flat-layout prompt-alignment metrics. Its initial programs already exceed all three baselines on mean BLIPScore, before critique or seed search. Retained programs have a median length of 21 lines when pooled across backbones. In a blind four-way comparison involving 30 participants and 20 prompts, our renders receive 59.2% of choices, compared with 19.3% for the most-preferred baseline. Compact executable programs thus offer a way to generate prompt-aligned materials while retaining their construction as part of the asset.

</details>


### [183] [I Have a Stream: Making Self-Supervised Learning Work on Continuous Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40333)
*Ivan Martinović,Lukas Knobel,Yuki M. Asano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究视频流式自监督预训练，构建WT++数据集并提出StreamMAE，弥合流式与i.i.d.预训练差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习灵感源于婴儿视觉发育，但标准训练流程中图像独立采样并跨epoch全局打乱，与真实视觉发展不符。作者希望研究连续视频流下的自监督学习，即帧按时间顺序、严格滑窗批次消费，无全局重排和多epoch重放。

Method: 构建WT++（95小时城市步行游览视频）流式预训练数据集及评估套件；分析对比学习和蒸馏方法、MAE在该设置下的表现；发现主要挑战是批次内高相似度（帧近似重复），并提出StreamMAE，保留MAE重建目标，引入流感知正则化与运动偏好裁剪选择。

Result: 对比学习和蒸馏方法在流式设置下表现不佳；MAE较稳健但仍不及标准i.i.d.预训练；批间高相似度不能解释差距，批内高相似度才是主要挑战。StreamMAE优于流式基线，匹配同视频数据i.i.d. MAE，与ImageNet预训练MAE相当，且随流从12增至95小时正向扩展。

Conclusion: 流式视频预训练的主要障碍是批内近重复帧，StreamMAE通过流感知正则化和运动偏好裁剪有效缓解该问题，实现与i.i.d.预训练相当的性能并具正向扩展性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning draws inspiration from infant visual development, yet standard training pipelines bear little resemblance to it: images are independently sampled and globally shuffled across epochs. We study self-supervised learning from continuous video streams, where frames are consumed in temporal order using strict sliding-window batches, without global reshuffling or multi-epoch replay. To this end, we construct WT++, a 95-hour urban walking-tour video dataset for streaming pretraining. Combined with a comprehensive evaluation suite we find that contrastive and distillation-based methods struggle in this setting, while MAE is more robust but still falls short of standard i.i.d. pretraining. We find that high inter-batch similarity, caused by sliding-window consumption across consecutive batches, does not explain this gap. The main challenge is high intra-batch similarity, where frames within each batch are near-duplicates. To mitigate this, we propose StreamMAE, which preserves the core MAE reconstruction objective while adapting the input pipeline with stream-aware regularization and motion-biased crop selection. StreamMAE outperforms streaming baselines, matches i.i.d. MAE trained on the same video data, remains competitive with ImageNet-pretrained MAE, and scales positively as the pretraining stream grows from 12 to 95 hours.

</details>


### [184] [Image Classifiers are Efficient Self-Supervised Video Representation Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40347)
*Owais Iqbal,Sudipta Sarkar,Shyam Marjit,Omprakash Chakraborty,Anirban Chakraborty,Abir Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoMSN框架，将视频视为由采样帧组成的"超图像"，用掩码孪生网络在图像ViT上高效学习视频时空表征，无需解码器与重建，性能达到SOTA且预训练轮数大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自监督方法依赖沉重的3D架构或基于重建的自编码器，计算成本高、效率低。作者希望借助图像基础模型，实现高效的视频时空表征学习。

Method: 将视频表示为超图像（采样帧构成的网格），从每个超图像构造两个视图：一个进行空间块掩码，另一个进行时间帧掩码，避免跨帧信息泄漏。共享ViT编码器通过掩码孪生损失对齐两个视图的嵌入，从而捕捉运动与外观线索，且不使用解码器。

Result: 基于预训练的DINO-v3与DeiT-v3图像编码器，VideoMSN在Kinetics-400、UCF101和HMDB51上达到SOTA，视频预训练轮数比以往方法分别减少最多32倍和160倍；在低样本分类场景中也表现强劲，证明所学表征的可迁移性。

Conclusion: VideoMSN表明，复用图像基础模型并结合掩码孪生学习，可实现高效且强泛化的视频自监督表征学习，适用于标签稀缺场景。

Abstract: We introduce VideoMSN, a Masked Siamese Network framework for efficient self-supervised spatio-temporal representation learning in videos. Instead of relying on heavy 3D architectures or reconstruction-based autoencoders for learning with unlabeled data, we repurpose standard image Vision Transformers by representing videos as super images which are grids composed of frames sampled from videos. From each super image, we construct two views: one with spatial patch masking and the other with temporal frame masking, ensuring no information leakage across frames. A shared Vision Transformer (ViT) encoder aligns their embeddings using a masked Siamese loss, capturing both motion and appearance cues without reconstruction. Our decoder-free formulation leverages an image foundation model towards efficient video representation learning. Starting from pretrained DINO-v3 and DeiT-v3 image encoders, VideoMSN achieves state-of-the-art performance on Kinetics-400, UCF101, and HMDB51 while requiring up to $32\times$ fewer and $160\times$ fewer video pretraining epochs compared to prior video self-supervised learning methods. Our proposed approach also shows strong performance in low-shot classification, confirming the transferability of the learned representations in a label-scarce scenario. Project Page: https://cvir.github.io/projects/videomsn.

</details>


### [185] [ViTeX-Bench: Benchmarking High-Fidelity Video Scene Text Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40356)
*Xinghao Chen,Xiangbo Gao,Jiongze Yu,Yuheng Wu,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ViTeX-Bench，一个面向视频场景文本编辑（video scene text editing）的基准套件，包含 ViTeX-Dataset 数据集与三轴评测协议，并发布开源参考编辑器 ViTeX-Edit-14B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成日益成熟，但视频编辑（尤其是需保留原场景动态的精确局部编辑）发展不足。视频场景文本编辑相比图像领域研究较少，缺少成对真实视频数据，且通用视频编辑指标无法直接衡量文本随时间的正确性。

Method: 构建 ViTeX-Bench：ViTeX-Dataset 含 387 个真实 720p 视频，带文本区域掩码与编辑指令，其中 230 个为经审核的流程生成成对编辑用于训练，157 个为冻结评测集。评测协议从文本正确性、视觉与时间质量、编辑局部性三轴出发，使用 13 项指标（每轴一个主指标）并进行帕累托权衡比较；辅以 OCR 校准、人工评测与标注敏感性分析。此外，基于成对训练集微调开源编辑器 ViTeX-Edit-14B，采用运动对齐的字形-视频条件。

Result: 在来自四个编辑家族的八个基线中，难以同时实现准确文本、时间稳定性与场景保持。ViTeX-Edit-14B 取得 CharAcc 0.688（评测的视频原生编辑器中均值最高），并在原始编辑器输出中拥有最低的可比文本裁剪 Warp。

Conclusion: ViTeX-Bench 为研究视频场景文本编辑中的上述权衡提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Recent video generation is increasingly realistic and controllable, yet video editing remains less developed, particularly for precise local edits that must preserve the original scene dynamics. Video scene text editing replaces text on scene surfaces, such as storefront signs, whiteboards, and product labels, while preserving the surrounding content, motion, and camera dynamics. Although scene text editing is well studied for images, video scene text editing that achieves high visual quality, temporal consistency, and edit locality remains underexplored. Existing resources offer limited paired real-video data, and general video-editing metrics do not directly measure whether the requested text remains correct over time. We introduce ViTeX-Bench, a benchmark suite comprising ViTeX-Dataset and a three-axis evaluation protocol. The dataset contains 387 real-world 720p videos with text-region masks and editing instructions: 230 provide reviewed, pipeline-generated paired edits for training, and 157 form a frozen evaluation split. The protocol evaluates text correctness, visual and temporal quality, and edit locality through 13 metrics, with one primary metric per axis and a Pareto comparison of their trade-offs. OCR calibration, human evaluation, and annotation-sensitivity analyses support the interpretation of these scores. Across eight baselines from four editing families, accurate text, temporal stability, and scene preservation remain difficult to achieve together. We also release ViTeX-Edit-14B, an open-source reference editor fine-tuned on the paired training split with motion-aligned glyph-video conditioning. It achieves CharAcc 0.688, the highest mean among the evaluated video-native editors, and the lowest comparable text-crop Warp among raw editor outputs. ViTeX-Bench provides a reproducible foundation for studying these trade-offs in video scene text editing.

</details>


### [186] [Physis-Lang: Self-Evolving Language as a Physical Representation for Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40358)
*Liming Lu,Xianzheng Ma,Wenkun He,Guanqi Zhan,Yilin Zhao,Junyu Chen,Mengyao Xu,Jiaojiao Fan,Wenhang Ge,Yuchao Gu,Yunze Liu,Boyi Li,Zhen Dong,Victor Prisacariu,Ming-Yu Liu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Physis-Lang，一个将"物理语言"作为共享可优化表示的自进化框架，统一用于数据整理、模型训练和视频生成，从而提升视频世界模型的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽能生成视觉上逼真的视频，却常常违反基本物理原理。主流方法认为自然语言不足以表达可靠生成所需的物理知识，因而额外引入视觉、潜变量、数值或规划信号。本文重新审视这一假设，探索仅用语言即可承载物理知识。

Method: 用语言描述物理过程的相关实体、成因、相互作用、支配原理、时间演化和效果，作为跨数据整理、训练与生成的共享表示。构建 PhysCapBench，将物理过程分解为原子断言，用召回率和精确率评估描述。通过智能体循环迭代分析断言级错误并优化生成物理描述的指令；同时把模型缺陷转化为文本描述，并用语言引导检索找出覆盖缺失物理过程的多样化视频。

Result: 在四个常用物理视频基准上，基于 Wan 和 Cosmos 骨干网络均取得一致的物理合理性提升。尤其是基于开源的 Cosmos3-Nano 骨干，Physis-Lang 增强后的模型超越了领先的专有模型 Veo 3.1。

Conclusion: 自然语言足以作为承载物理知识的共享可优化表示，无需依赖额外模态信号；以物理语言为中心的自进化框架能有效提升视频生成的物理合理性，并使开源模型达到甚至超越专有模型水平。

Abstract: Video world models are expected to predict how the physical world evolves, yet they often produce visually plausible videos that violate basic physical principles. Existing approaches commonly assume that natural language is insufficient to represent the physical knowledge required for reliable generation, and therefore introduce additional visual, latent, numerical, or planning-based signals. We revisit this assumption and introduce Physis-Lang, a self-evolving framework that treats physical language as a shared and optimizable representation across data curation, model training, and video generation. Physis-Lang represents physical processes through language that describes their relevant entities, causes, interactions, governing principles, temporal evolution, and effects. To improve this representation, we construct PhysCapBench, which decomposes physical processes into atomic assertions and evaluates captions using recall and precision. An agentic loop iteratively analyzes assertion-level errors and refines the instruction used to produce physical captions. Physis-Lang further converts model deficiencies into textual descriptions and uses language-guided retrieval to identify visually diverse videos that cover missing physical processes. Experiments on four widely used physical video benchmarks with Wan and Cosmos backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in physical plausibility. Notably, starting from open-source Cosmos3-Nano backbones, our Physis-Lang-enhanced models surpass the leading proprietary Veo 3.1 model.

</details>


### [187] [Multimodal Flow: Unified Flow Modeling of Language and Vision in Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40362)
*Hongyuan Tao,Xinggang Wang,Lianghui Zhu,Yongkang Li,Yunchao Wei,Bin Feng,Shaoyu Chen,Qian Zhang,Chang Huang,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Multimodal Flow（MF-1），一种全连续的统一多模态生成模型，将文本块与图像作为有序连续"超块"（hyperchunks），通过共享的 chunk-causal flow backbone 与 Flow Matching 学习统一向量场，实现语言与视觉的联合建模，在低训练数据预算下达到有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态统一模型存在两类权衡：一类将语言与量化图像都建模为离散 token，带来视觉量化瓶颈；另一类将离散语言预测与连续图像生成结合，却需要模态相关的目标和采样流程。全连续建模可避免这些权衡并共享生成过程，但在多模态预训练中仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出统一连续架构，将多模态连续表示与共享的 chunk-causal flow backbone 结合。把文本块和图像组织为有序连续超块，保留文本 token 顺序与视觉空间结构；骨干通过 Flow Matching 在这些超块上学习单一向量场。联合注意力实现跨模态交互，模态专属前馈网络分别处理各模态。训练时并行预测多个目标块，推理时顺序生成超块。实例化模型 MF-1 并在多模态数据上预训练。

Result: 在 0.6B、1.2B、1.6B 三个规模上，继续预训练持续提升多模态建模能力。仅用 150B 预训练 token，MF-1 在 GenEval 与 DPG-Bench 上平均得分 82.8，在 VQAv2、MMBench 与 POPE 上平均 75.3，与使用远多数据训练的统一模型相比仍具竞争力。在相同数据、优化与参数预算下，Multimodal Flow 进一步优于代表性的混合模型和离散模型。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于连续块的嵌入流建模可作为一种全新的全连续范式用于统一多模态建模。相关代码与模型已公开于 https://github.com/hustvl/Multimodal-Flow。

Abstract: We present Multimodal Flow, a fully continuous generative model of language and vision. Most unified multimodal models either model both language and quantized images as discrete tokens or combine discrete language prediction with continuous image generation. The former introduces a visual quantization bottleneck. The latter requires modality-dependent objectives and sampling procedures. Fully continuous modeling avoids these trade-offs and enables a shared generative process, but remains underexplored for multimodal pretraining. Multimodal Flow introduces a unified continuous architecture that integrates multimodal continuous representations with a shared chunk-causal flow backbone. It organizes text blocks and images as ordered continuous hyperchunks, preserving textual token order and visual spatial structure. The backbone learns a single vector field over these hyperchunks through Flow Matching. Joint attention enables cross-modal interaction, while modality-specific feed-forward networks process each modality. The model predicts multiple target chunks in parallel during training and generates hyperchunks sequentially at inference. We instantiate MF-1 and pretrain it on multimodal data. Across 0.6B, 1.2B, and 1.6B scales, continued pretraining consistently improves multimodal modeling. With only 150B pretraining tokens, MF-1 achieves an average score of 82.8 across GenEval and DPG-Bench and 75.3 across VQAv2, MMBench, and POPE, remaining competitive with unified models trained on substantially more data. Under matched data, optimization, and parameter budgets, Multimodal Flow further outperforms representative hybrid and discrete models. These results establish continuous chunk-based embedding flow modeling as a new fully continuous paradigm for unified multimodal modeling. The related code and model are publicly released at https://github.com/hustvl/Multimodal-Flow.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Optimize, Learn, Refine: Whole-Body Grasping and Pick-and-Throw with a Spiral Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38202)
*Marwah Basuhai,Tingcong Liu,Ibrahim Alsarraj,Yuhao Wang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过基于结果的驱动空间优化，实现软连续体机器人从初始未抓取状态出发的全身抓取与抓取投掷，仿真中抓取成功率达98.4%，硬件实验中抓取与投掷成功率均为100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软连续体机器人可利用分布式柔顺性进行全身操作，但通过改变接触来合成行为仍然困难。作者希望在不指定接触力、接触位置或身体构型的情况下，让抓取、加速与释放从柔顺交互中自发涌现。

Method: 将抓取量化为末端角扫掠与身体—物体包络，投掷进一步引入释放方向对齐与最小释放速度；针对驱动到结果映射非光滑的问题，在"优化—学习—精化"框架中使用无导数CMA-ES：CMA-ES为采样条件生成解，任务条件预测器学习热启动，再对未见条件用CMA-ES精化。

Result: 仿真中完成492/500次成功抓取（98.4%），三个方向投掷成功率分别为98%、97%、94%；学习初始化将抓取成功率从78.6%提升至98.4%，并使CMA-ES中位回滚次数从1184降至816。硬件实验中50次执行抓取成功率100%，30次执行抓取投掷成功率100%（每个方向重复10次）。

Conclusion: 仿真与硬件结果共同表明，所提框架在仿真和物理全身操作任务中均有效，验证了基于结果的驱动空间优化在软连续体机器人复杂操作中的可行性。

Abstract: Soft continuum robots can exploit distributed compliance for whole-body manipulation, but synthesizing behavior through changing contacts remains difficult. We address whole-body grasping and pick-and-throw from an initially ungrasped state through outcome-based actuation-space optimization. Grasping is quantified by tip angular sweep and body-object enclosure, while throwing further incorporates release-direction alignment and minimum release speed. These objectives allow grasping, acceleration, and release to emerge from compliant interaction without prescribing contact forces, contact locations, or body configurations. Because the resulting actuation-to-outcome mapping is nonsmooth, we utilize derivative-free CMA-ES within an optimize-learn-refine framework. CMA-ES generates solutions for sampled conditions, a task-conditioned predictor learns warm starts, and CMA-ES refines them for unseen conditions. In simulation, the method achieves 492/500 successful grasps (98.4%) and success rates of 98%, 97%, and 94% across three directional throwing trials. Learned initialization increases grasping success from 78.6% to 98.4% while reducing the median rollout count from 1184 to 816 in CMA-ES. Hardware experiments achieve a 100% grasping success rate across 50 executions and a 100% pick-and-throw success rate across 30 executions, with 10 repetitions per direction. Together, these simulation and hardware results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across both simulated and physical whole-body manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [189] [Fiatlux: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Humanoid Ladder Climbing and Light-Bulb Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38216)
*Pavel Bushuyeu,Yujin Chen,Anton Nikolaev,Brian Shu,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fiatlux基准，基于Isaac Lab，在单一长时程回合中评测人形机器人完成换灯泡任务（搬梯、攀爬、换灯泡、丢弃旧灯泡），含12个子任务环境与难度加权评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将桌面操作、平地家务或人形行走操作分开评测，缺少在单一长时程回合中同时考察垂直移动与对易碎负载的灵巧操作的任务。

Method: 构建Unitree G1人形机器人的换灯泡长时程任务，分解为12个子任务环境，采用难度加权门控打分；观测分为标准模式（真实可感知信号）与特权模式（精确仿真状态）；提供RSL-RL PPO、零样本GR00T N1.7 VLA及全身控制器等基线，并提供遥操作录制用于设定与校验成功门控。

Result: 给出评测协议与多类参考基线实现，并公开基准代码与遥操作录制数据。

Conclusion: Fiatlux为垂直移动与易碎负载灵巧操作的联合长时程评测提供了统一平台与可复现基线。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks evaluate tabletop manipulation, flat-floor household activity, or humanoid locomotion and manipulation as separate task groups; none scores vertical mobility and dexterous work on a fragile payload in one long-horizon episode. We present Fiatlux, a light-bulb replacement benchmark built on NVIDIA Isaac Lab. In one episode, a Unitree G1 humanoid positions a step ladder under a ceiling or wall fixture, climbs it, exchanges a spent bulb in a socket for a fresh one, and leaves the spent one in a disposal crate. We decompose the episode into twelve subtask environments scored on difficulty-weighted gates. The goal is a successful replacement, with the fresh bulb seated, the spent one disposed of, neither dropped, and a fragility bound not crossed. Runs that fall short can earn partial credit. Observations are split into a standard mode (signals a physical robot could sense or estimate) and a privileged mode (exact simulator state). We specify the evaluation protocol and provide reference baseline implementations spanning RSL-RL PPO, zero-shot NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and whole-body controllers. Additionally, we provide the teleoperated recordings used to specify and check the success gates. The benchmark code and the teleoperated recordings are available at fiatlux-bench.github.io.

</details>


### [190] [SynIL: Leveraging Synergy for Offline Imitation Learning from Imperfect Demonstration Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38225)
*Yuto Tanaka,Kyo Kutsuzawa,Martina Doku,Dai Owaki,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SynIL框架，基于运动协同（motor synergy）实现无标签、自动化的示教质量评估，生成密集的transition级奖励信号，在D4RL和Robomimic上显著优于BC并可与使用真实奖励的离线RL媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习虽能从大量示教数据中直接获取复杂技能，但当数据被次优或噪声示教污染时性能急剧下降。现有质量评估方法依赖人工预选专家参考数据或任务特定启发式规则，扩展性差。因此需要一种自动化、无需标签的示教质量评估方法。

Method: 受神经科学证据启发——运动协同（运动中的低维协调结构）与运动熟练度直接相关，SynIL通过算法量化协同的表现程度，并利用自监督奖励回归生成密集的、transition级别的奖励信号。

Result: 在D4RL locomotion基准和多人类Robomimic操作数据集上的综合评估表明，协同导出的奖励与真实奖励高度相关；SynIL大幅优于Behavior Cloning（BC），性能可与使用真实环境奖励训练的离线RL相当，在稀疏奖励的人类遥操作场景中甚至更优。

Conclusion: SynIL提供了一种自动化、无标签的示教质量评估方案，利用运动协同生成密集奖励，有效提升受污染数据下的模仿学习性能，且无需人工预选或任务特定启发式。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to acquire complex skills directly from massive demonstration datasets, but its performance degrades severely when datasets are contaminated with suboptimal or noisy demonstrations. While prior quality-assessment methods attempt to filter or reweight data, they typically rely on manual pre-selection of expert reference data or task-specific heuristics, limiting scalability. To address this challenge, we introduce SynIL (Synergy-based Imitation Learning), a novel framework for automated, label-free demonstration quality assessment in offline reinforcement learning. Grounded in neuroscientific evidence that motor synergy, a low-dimensional coordinated structure in movement, correlates directly with motor proficiency, SynIL algorithmically quantifies synergy manifestation to generate dense, transition-level reward signals via self-supervised reward regression. Comprehensive evaluations on D4RL locomotion benchmarks and multi-human Robomimic manipulation datasets demonstrate that synergy-derived rewards correlate strongly with ground-truth rewards. Furthermore, SynIL substantially outperforms Behavior Cloning (BC) and achieves performance comparable to, and in sparse-reward human teleoperation scenarios, superior to, offline reinforcement learning trained on true environment rewards.

</details>


### [191] [A Two-Echelon Covering Tour Vehicle Routing Problem with Drones for Post-Disaster Relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38227)
*Dang Viet Anh Nguyen,Rajesh Piplani,Aldy Gunawan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两级覆盖巡游车辆路径问题（2E-CTVRP），用于灾后救援物资配送，建立混合整数线性规划模型，并设计混合元启发式算法 GRASP-ILS-PR 求解，在 110 个基准实例上验证其高效性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后救援物资配送需要兼顾运输效率与受灾点覆盖，传统车辆路径模型难以刻画卡车与无人机协同的两级配送结构，因此需要建立新的问题模型并开发高效求解算法。

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数线性规划；第一级用卡车将物资和无人机从中心仓库运至灾区外围卫星点，第二级由卫星点并行发射无人机向聚类后受灾点质心配送，卡车在卫星点等待无人机返回；决策包括卫星点与卡车的分配、卡车路径排序、受灾簇与卫星点的分配；提出混合元启发式 GRASP-ILS-PR，结合贪婪随机构造、单轨迹搜索和针对簇-卫星分配的周期性路径重连。

Result: 在 110 个新基准实例上，GRASP-ILS-PR 在 30 秒内找到所有已知最优解；在多数较大实例上匹配或优于商业求解器一小时解，在 50 个卫星点的实例上最多改进 11%；明显优于采用演化路径重连的传统 GRASP，且比使用相同搜索组件的模拟退火结果更稳定。两种车队配置分析表明：少数大型卡车在多数实例中最小化累计到达时间，而多辆小型卡车在每个实例中都能更早且更公平地向受灾者送达物资。

Conclusion: 该研究为灾后两级卡车-无人机协同救援配送提供了有效建模与求解框架，GRASP-ILS-PR 在求解质量和稳定性上表现优异，车队配置分析为救援决策中的公平性与时效性权衡提供了参考。

Abstract: We introduce the two-echelon covering tour vehicle routing problem (2E-CTVRP) for the distribution of relief supplies after a disaster. In the first echelon, a fleet of trucks transports supplies and drones from a central depot to satellites at the periphery of the affected area. In the second echelon, drones launched in parallel from the satellites deliver the supplies to the centroids of victim clusters, which are obtained by clustering the victim locations, and each truck waits at a satellite until its drones have returned. The problem combines the assignment of satellites to trucks, the sequencing of the truck routes, and the assignment of clusters to satellites, and minimizes the sum of the arrival times of the trucks at the satellites and at the depot. We formulate the 2E-CTVRP as a mixed integer linear program and propose a hybrid metaheuristic, GRASP-ILS-PR, which combines greedy randomized construction, a single-trajectory search, and periodic path relinking on the assignment of clusters to satellites. On a new benchmark set of 110 instances, GRASP-ILS-PR finds all known optimal solutions within 30 seconds, matches or improves on the one-hour solutions of a commercial solver on most larger instances, and improves on them by up to 11% for instances with 50 satellites. It clearly outperforms a conventional GRASP with evolutionary path relinking and yields more consistent results than a simulated annealing algorithm that uses the same search components. An analysis of two fleet configurations shows that few large trucks minimize the cumulative arrival time in most instances, whereas many small trucks deliver supplies to the victims earlier and more equitably in every instance.

</details>


### [192] [TALK-Dem: Benchmarking Embodied Task Planning under Dementia-Associated Communication Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38371)
*Guangxin Zhao,Yiran Hu,Yuan Cao,Chenxi Jiang,Jianfei Yang,Yegang Du,Yasuyuki Taki,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Lin Gu,Zhi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TALK-Dem基准，评估LLM驱动机器人任务规划在痴呆相关言语沟通下的鲁棒性，并提出CARE方法进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器人任务规划假设用户指令清晰完整，但面对真实用户（尤其是认知障碍者如痴呆患者）时容易出错甚至带来物理安全风险。

Method: 构建TALK-Dem基准，包含4800条指令，覆盖5种沟通模式（指称不精确、物体替换、空洞言语、话题漂移、侵入）和3个强度等级；在6个开源LLM上测试，并提出Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience (CARE)方法，检索以往已解决的相关任务以提供任务特定解释与规划上下文。

Result: 开源模型在非理想指令下性能最多下降22.3个百分点；CARE在6个模型上平均任务成功率比原始提示提升18.1个百分点，普遍优于标准提示基线。

Conclusion: 强调了评估沟通鲁棒性及开发本地可部署辅助机器人适应策略的重要性，数据集已公开。

Abstract: Existing LLM-driven robot task planners rely on a taken-for-granted assumption of an ideal user whose instructions are clear, complete, and task-focused. However, when interacting with real-world users, especially those experiencing cognitive impairments, such as people living with dementia (PLWD), the planners often make mistakes and even pose physical safety risks. We proposed TALK-Dem (Talking Attributes and Linguistic Knowledge in Dementia), the first benchmark for evaluating LLM-driven robot task planning under dementia-associated verbal communication. TALK-Dem contains 4,800 instructions and covers five typical communication patterns, including Referential Imprecision, Object Substitution, Empty Speech, Topic Drift, and Intrusion, at three intensity levels. Experiments across six open-weight LLMs reveal a substantial robustness gap. Across communication patterns, open-weight models exhibited performance drops of up to 22.3 percentage points compared to ideal instructions. This revealed a critical gap and even danger for real-world applications, especially in assistive robotics, where locally deployable models are necessary due to privacy concerns and connectivity constraints. To mitigate this issue, we proposed the Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience (CARE) method, which retrieves relevant previously resolved tasks to provide task-specific interpretation and planning context. CARE generally outperformed standard prompting baselines across the six open-weight models, improving average task success by 18.1 percentage points over the vanilla prompt. These results highlighted the importance of both evaluating communication robustness and developing effective adaptation strategies for locally deployable assistive robots. The TALK-Dem dataset is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TALK-Dem-A6B3/.

</details>


### [193] [GestAdapt: Workspace-Conditioned Co-Speech Gesture Generation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38400)
*Bosong Ding,Xianglin Zhang,Miao Xin,Murat Kirtay,Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GestAdapt 框架，将协同语音手势生成本身条件化于给定的手腕工作空间，而非事后修正，实验证明在工作空间约束下生成的手势质量与自然度优于修正无约束轨迹的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人做协同语音手势时不仅要适配语音与本体结构，还要适配可用的动作工作空间（如靠近墙面时）。由于同一句话可对应不同手势，机器人应直接生成符合工作空间约束的合适动作，而不是先做无约束生成再修正；后者可能损害手势自然度。

Method: 提出 GestAdapt，一个以工作空间为条件的协同语音手势生成框架，将生成过程条件化于预设的手腕工作空间。框架通过共享的运动表示从六个互补的协同语音语料库中学习，并支持向不同机器人本体进行重定向。评估采用定量分析、用户研究以及在 Reachy2 人形机器人上的真实机器人实验（在相同工作空间约束下对比各方法）。

Result: 定量评估显示生成动作既接近真实动作分布又能遵守工作空间约束。用户研究中，修改工作空间约束下生成的手势平均质量得分为 3.24/5，高于无工作空间变体（2.43/5），低于参考动作（3.68/5）。真实机器人评估中，GestAdapt 生成的动作在 69.7% 的对比中排名第一，优于无工作空间基线以及事后约束的重定向真值动作。

Conclusion: 结果表明，应在手势生成过程中就适配可用工作空间，而非事后修正无约束轨迹来满足工作空间约束，后者可能损害手势的自然性。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures for robots must adapt not only to speech and embodiment, but also to the workspace available for performing the motion. Since the same speech can be accompanied by different gestures, a robot can respond to workspace constraints, e.g., gestures for speech next to a wall. In these scenarios, the robot should gesture in a suitable motion rather than simply correcting an unconstrained one. To achieve this goal, we present GestAdapt, a workspace-conditioned framework that conditions co-speech gesture generation on a prescribed wrist workspace. The GestAdapt framework learns from six complementary co-speech corpora through a shared motion representation and supports retargeting to different robot embodiments. Quantitative evaluation shows that generated motions remain close to the real-motion distribution while respecting the workspace. In a user study, gestures generated under modified workspace constraints receive a mean quality score of 3.24/5, above our no-workspace variant (2.43/5) and below the reference motions (3.68/5). In a real robot evaluation, all compared motions are retargeted to the Reachy2 humanoid robot under identical workspace constraints. Motions generated with our framework rank first in 69.7\% of comparisons, higher than our no-workspace variant baseline and retargeted ground-truth motions constrained afterward. Overall, the results support adapting gestures to the available workspace during generation, rather than modifying unconstrained trajectories afterward to satisfy workspace constraints, potentially compromising gesture naturalness.

</details>


### [194] [Memorize, Adapt, Ignore: Diagnosing Robot Learning Mechanisms under Training Data Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38401)
*Ke Zhang,Danica J. Sutherland,Chao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过实证神经正切核(NTK)等工具，系统研究训练数据变化（域随机化DR与模仿学习演示）提升机器人操作策略鲁棒性的内在机制，并给出DR设计、模型选择与捷径学习检测的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据变化是提升机器人操作策略鲁棒性的主要手段，但其内在机制仍不清晰，实践中往往依赖昂贵的试错来选取随机化参数。论文希望揭示这些机制，从而指导实践。

Method: 围绕物体大小、颜色、类型，以及场景光照和语言提示等进行一系列案例研究；覆盖ManiSkill中的抓取-放置强化学习，以及在LIBERO和RoboTwin上微调视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型。同时考察模型行为与内部表征，以实证神经正切核(NTK)作为主要诊断工具。

Result: NTK能够区分内部学习机制的转变：在变化不足时表现为对不同情形的"记忆"（如分别学习大立方体和小立方体的做法），在变化充分时表现为对当前情形的"适应"。此外，基于NTK的信噪比可判别策略是否学会了忽略任务无关因素（如将蓝、红立方体同等对待，而非学习蓝色子策略与红色子策略）。

Conclusion: 这些诊断工具可用于制定域随机化方案设计、模型选择与捷径学习检测的实用指南；论文还比较了不同表征，并通过基于ACT模仿学习的真实硬件实验验证了所得结论。

Abstract: Training data variation, whether through designing a domain randomization (DR) scheme in simulation or curating demonstrations for imitation learning, is a primary lever for improving the robustness of robotic manipulation policies. Yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood, and practitioners typically select randomization parameters through expensive trial and error. We investigate these mechanisms through a series of case studies, randomizing object size, color, and type as well as scene lighting and linguistic prompts across settings including pick-and-place RL in ManiSkill and fine-tuning of vision-language-action (VLA) models on LIBERO and RoboTwin. We examine both model behavior and internal representations, using the empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) as our primary diagnostic tool. We show that the NTK distinguishes a shift in the internal learning mechanism from \textit{memorizing} different situations with insufficient variation (e.g.\ learning what to do for a large cube, and what to do for a small cube) to \textit{adapting} to the situation at hand with sufficient variation. An NTK-based signal-to-noise ratio also helps distinguish when policies have learned to \emph{ignore} task-irrelevant factors (e.g.\ treating blue and red cubes identically, instead of learning a blue sub-policy and a red sub-policy). We use these diagnostics to develop practical guidance for designing DR schemes, selecting models, and detecting shortcut learning. We further compare different kinds of representations and validate our findings with real-world hardware experiments using ACT-based imitation learning.

</details>


### [195] [Draft: A Parametric Tool for Robot Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38405)
*David Nguyen,Marcelo Coelho,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Draft，一种参数化生成工具，可将任意串行链参数树编译为可直接仿真的 MJCF 模型，无需 CAD，并通过真实硬件数据趋势约束设计参数，用于探索形态与控制协同设计中的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人性能受限于形态与控制联合迭代的高成本：每次 CAD 修改都需先转为仿真可用模型才能开始控制工作；现有协同设计方法多为单一平台构建的模型生成器，且缺乏真实世界数据支撑设计合理性。

Method: Draft 是一个参数化生成工具，其通用引擎可将任意串行链参数树直接编译为仿真就绪的 MJCF 模型，无需 CAD；同时通过拟合 114 个执行器和 49 个已发表机器人描述的趋势，为每个设计的自由参数提供真实硬件依据。

Result: 通过为四款现成机器人构建数字孪生验证趋势，质量与真实值达到 1.10 倍几何平均折叠误差；并通过两阶段强化学习课程评估三款四足机器人，展示 Draft 如何揭示设计权衡。

Conclusion: Draft 提供了一种无需 CAD、面向真实硬件的参数化机器人生成方案，能有效支持形态与控制协同设计中的权衡探索与控制器性能评估。

Abstract: Robot performance is often limited by the cost of iterating on morphology and control together, since every computer-aided design (CAD) change has to be carried into a simulation-ready model before control work begins. Co-design methods attempt to close this gap, but each uses a model generator written for a single platform or lack the use of real-world data to suggest that designs are plausible. We present Draft, a parametric generation tool whose generalized engine compiles any parametric tree of serial chains into a simulation-ready MJCF model, without CAD. It allows engineers to explore design tradeoffs through easily adjustable models and evaluate how changes influence controller performance. Draft grounds the free parameters of each design using trends fitted to a survey of $114$ actuators and $49$ published robot descriptions, so that a generated robot is anchored to real-world hardware. We validate those trends wholistically by building twins of four off-the-shelf robots, whose masses agree to $1.10\times$ geometric mean fold error. Finally, we demonstrate how Draft exposes design tradeoffs by evaluating three quadrupeds through a two-stage reinforcement learning curriculum.

</details>


### [196] [PneuTac: Tactile Manipulation with Soft Pneumatic Robots via Unified MPM-Gaussian Splatting Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38418)
*Shaohong Zhong,Marco Pontin,Joe Watson,Perla Maiolino,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PneuTac，一个面向软体气动机器人触觉反馈操作的统一仿真与学习框架，结合 MPM 物理建模与 3DGS 渲染，实现高精度 real-to-sim 建模并支撑策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体气动机器人与视触觉传感器在精细操作中潜力巨大，但触觉操作学习受限于高效仿真的缺失：现有仿真器通常将二者孤立建模，且存在难以高效弥合的标定差距。

Method: 采用物质点法（MPM）对软体机器人及可变形触觉膜进行动力学建模，用 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行渲染；通过简单的视觉方法完成 real-to-sim 建模，并训练动作与感知网络作为代理模型以加速仿真；基于该框架构建触觉引导流程在仿真中采集演示数据。

Result: 在自研的带触觉感知指尖的气动软指及额外跨设备评估中，PneuTac 能准确建模带触觉传感器的软体机器人；在三个真实世界接触密集的柔顺操作任务上，用仿真增强演示训练的策略优于仅用相同真实数据训练的基线。

Conclusion: PneuTac 是一个面向柔顺硬件的触觉操作实用框架，验证了统一仿真对提升软体触觉操作策略性能的有效性。

Abstract: Soft robots and tactile sensors have demonstrated great potential in delicate manipulation tasks. Soft pneumatic robots enable safe contact through compliance, and vision-based tactile sensors offer high-resolution touch perception. However, learning tactile manipulation with compliant robots has been challenging, bottlenecked by the lack of efficient simulation. Existing simulators typically model them in isolation, and exhibit large calibration gaps that are difficult to overcome efficiently. We present PneuTac, a unified framework for tactile-feedback manipulation with soft pneumatic robots. We leverage the material point method (MPM) for modelling the dynamics of the soft robot and the deformable tactile membrane, and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) for rendering. Real-to-sim modelling is done with a simple vision-based method, to then train action and perception networks for efficient simulation with surrogate models. We use the framework to drive a tactile-guided pipeline to collect demonstrations in simulation. Through experiments on a custom-designed pneumatic soft finger with a tactile sensing tip, together with additional cross-device evaluations, we show that PneuTac is capable of accurately modelling soft robots with tactile sensors, and that policies trained with simulation-augmented demonstrations outperform baselines trained on the same real data on three real-world contact-rich compliant manipulation tasks, making it a practical framework for tactile manipulation on compliant hardware.

</details>


### [197] [A Reachability-based Safety Certificate for Dynamical System Motion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38421)
*Aditya Vats,Tianyi Xia,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于后向可达管（backward reachability tube）的价值函数，用于认证动力系统（DS）策略在未知环境中的安全性，并将其作为控制障碍函数（CBF）的安全证书，避免调制与几何CBF策略在特定场景下的碰撞与伪吸引子问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DS是带有稳定性与收敛性理论保证的矢量场策略，但在未知环境中部署时需局部重塑（调制或几何控制障碍函数）。然而受障碍物几何形状和DS复杂度影响，这些局部策略可能导致不可避免的碰撞或伪吸引子。现有的安全认证方法通常因维度灾难而难以求解，因此需要一种可行且严格的安全认证方法。

Method: 将安全性认证问题建模为后向可达管，定义衡量标称DS轨迹最坏情况安全性的价值函数。指出在DS-based学习示范（LfD）场景下：无控制输入使可达性问题退化为确定性rollout，特定稳定性条件将无限时域截断为有限时域，从而得到良定义的价值函数。进一步证明该价值函数是标称DS流在无障碍区域内的最大前向不变子集，并将其用作控制障碍函数（CBF-on-V）。

Result: 在五种DS构造上验证了该证书函数的有效性：解析DS、Neural ODE、微分同胚潜空间、LPV-DS、SE(3)；并在Franka机械臂上进行了实际验证。结果表明调制与几何CBF在正面接近不安全区域时存在鞍点问题，而CBF-on-V完全避免了这一陷阱。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于后向可达管的价值函数为DS提供了一种可计算、有理论保证的安全认证方法，克服了传统局部重塑策略的碰撞与伪吸引子缺陷，并在多种DS构造及真实机械臂上验证了其有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Dynamical Systems (DS) are reactive motion policies representing vector fields trained with theoretical guarantees of stability and convergence. To ensure safety during deployment in unknown environments they must be locally reshaped, either through modulation or geometric control barrier function strategies. However, depending on the geometry of the obstacles and the complexity of the DS, these local strategies can lead the system to unavoidable collisions or spurious attractors. In this work, we certify safety with a value function drawn from the notion of backward reachability tube, which measures the worst-case safety along a rollout trajectory of the nominal DS. Usually, such a value function is intractable for a controlled system due to curse of dimensionality. We show that in the DS-based learning-from-demonstration setting, the absence of a control input collapses the reachability problem to a deterministic rollout, and the presence of certain stability conditions truncates the infinite horizon to a finite one, resulting in a well-defined value function. We further show that the value function we devised is the maximal forward-invariant subset of the obstaclefree region for the nominal DS flow. The application of this certificate function is validated across five DS constructions - analytical, Neural ODE, diffeomorphic latent space, LPV-DS, SE(3)and validate it on a Franka manipulator. Modulation and geometric CBFs also suffer from saddle point in cases of headon approach towards an unsafe zone. We show that CBF-on-V avoids this pitfall entirely.

</details>


### [198] [Embodiment-aware control by inference over the operator: a simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38437)
*Sara Falcone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通用具身引擎（UEE），一种基于离散主动推理的控制器，通过隐式眼动与瞳孔信号推断操作者具身感及视觉-本体感觉权重，从而在显式偏好下选择有界设备设置，在300个异质合成操作者的仿真中多数在半分钟内找到合适设置，接近预言机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作系统针对信道保真度调参，而操作者是否将设备体验为身体一部分（具身感SoE）只在事后通过问卷测量。预测加工理论主张应减少操作者预测与反馈间的失配，但这些预测不可观测，且仅惩罚失配的目标可通过移除反馈来最小化。因此需要一个能推断具身感并选择设备设置的控制器。

Method: 将具身感控制器UEE建模为离散主动推理智能体：从隐式注视和瞳孔信号及任务结果推断操作者的具身感与视觉-本体感觉线索权重，在显式偏好下选择有界设备设置。与无模型赌博机、仅推断的期望效用控制器、朴素预测误差最小化器等基线进行对比。

Result: 在300个异质合成操作者仿真中，UEE对多数操作者在半分钟内找到合适设置（尚未识别其精确类型），会话后半段接近预言机（具身感1.68对最佳固定设置0.73，0-2量表）。不读取操作者的自适应与固定控制相当，无模型赌博机更差，而具有相同推断的期望效用控制器表现完全相同。朴素预测误差最小化器会扣留反馈，任务成功率下降（0.74对0.90）。

Conclusion: 收益来自对操作者的贝叶斯推断与显式偏好，而非主动推理的信息寻求项。对模型族之外的操作者收益缩小但持续存在，随群体多样性增加而增大；当控制器信任无信息信号或未建模的线索权重中途变化时收益消失。这些失败表明以人为被试的研究须首先确立标定信号与变化模型。

Abstract: Teleoperation systems are tuned for channel fidelity, while whether the operator experiences the device as part of the body, the Sense of Embodiment (SoE), is measured only afterwards, by questionnaire. Predictive-processing accounts suggest controlling devices to reduce the mismatch between the operator's predictions and the returned feedback, but those predictions are unobservable, and an objective that only penalizes mismatch is minimized by removing feedback. We formulate an embodiment-aware controller, the Universal Embodiment Engine (UEE), that infers the operator's embodiment and visuo-proprioceptive cue weighting from implicit gaze and pupil signals and task outcome, and chooses bounded device settings under explicit preferences, cast as a discrete Active Inference agent. In simulations with 300 heterogeneous synthetic operators, the UEE found the suitable setting within half a minute for most operators, before identifying their exact type, and came close to an oracle in the second half of the session (embodiment 1.68 against 0.73 for the best fixed setting, on a 0-2 scale). Adapting without reading the operator did no better than fixed control, and model-free bandits did worse, whereas an expected-utility controller with the same inference did exactly as well: the benefit comes from Bayesian inference over the operator with explicit preferences, not from the information-seeking term of Active Inference. A naive prediction-error minimizer withheld feedback, as its objective implies, and lost task success (0.74 vs 0.90). The benefit shrank but persisted for operators outside the controller's model family, grew with the variety of the population, and vanished when the controller trusted an uninformative signal or when cue weighting changed mid-session without being modeled. These failures show what studies with people must establish first: calibrated signals and a model of change.

</details>


### [199] [BIND: Binding 3D Robot Actions to 2D Image Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38443)
*Cameron Smith,Arsh Tangri,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang,Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIND 提出一种将 3D 机器人动作与对应 2D 图像特征"绑定"的动作表示，利用相机几何而非学习来建立动作-特征关系，从而在真实机器人上实现高数据效率和强泛化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略通常将动作头设计为从单个全局特征向量到动作的 MLP 回归。这种全局表示要求策略网络仅凭演示自行发现目标动作与其投影图像特征之间的关系，导致策略对相机视角和目标位置的细微变化脆弱，且出乎意料地数据低效。

Method: BIND 通过相机几何而非学习来提供动作-特征关系：离散化候选末端执行器位置的体素体积，将每个候选位置与其在各相机视图中投影处的预训练特征关联；再对每个候选位置的坐标与图像绑定特征组合进行打分，从而选出动作。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中发现 BIND 高度数据高效且鲁棒：在少至 5 个演示的任务上实现近乎完美的成功率，并在剧烈的相机视角偏移和留出对象位置上表现优雅退化，而坐标回归基线在这些情形下完全失败；同时还考察了长时程任务执行和灵巧性。

Conclusion: BIND 用相机几何替代从数据中学习动作-特征对应关系，显著提升视觉运动策略的数据效率和对未见物体位置、相机视角的鲁棒性，优于依赖全局特征的坐标回归基线。

Abstract: We introduce BIND, a new action representation for visuomotor robot policies that binds 3D robot actions to their corresponding 2D image features, yielding strong data efficiency gains and robustness to out-of-distribution object positions and camera viewpoints. The action heads of current robot policies are typically formulated as an MLP regression from a single global feature vector produced by a pre-trained vision encoder. This global formulation requires the policy network to discover, from demonstrations alone, the relationship between target robot actions and the image features they project onto. The consequence is that although modern image features are semantically descriptive, spatially robust, and even multiview-consistent, the policies built on them are brittle to subtle changes in camera viewpoint and object placement--and surprisingly data-inefficient. BIND closes this gap by supplying the action-feature relationship through camera geometry rather than learning: it discretizes a volume of candidate end effector positions, attaches each candidate to the pre-trained features at its projection in each camera view, and selects actions by scoring each candidate's position and image-bound feature combination. On a real robot, we study data efficiency and out-of-distribution robustness to unseen object positions and camera viewpoints, as well as general long-horizon task execution and dexterity. We find BIND to be highly data-efficient and robust: it achieves near-perfect success on tasks with as few as 5 demonstrations, and degrades gracefully under steep camera-viewpoint shifts and held-out object positions where coordinate-regression baselines completely fail.

</details>


### [200] [Observability Analysis and Online Calibration of Visual-Inertial-Wheel Odometry for 4WIS4WID Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38462)
*Branimir Ćaran,Vladimir Milić,Bojan Šekoranja,Bojan Jerbić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向四轮独立转向驱动(4WIS4WID)移动机器人的视觉-惯性-轮式里程计(VIWO)框架，具备在线标定能力。基于四轮纵向滚动与横向无滑移约束推导2D里程计模型，并给出预积分模型与解析雅可比；通过可观测性分析证明冗余模型可恢复转向偏置的可观测性，并辨识退化运动及相应激励条件，仿真与实机实验验证了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4WIS4WID机器人各轮可独立转向与驱动，轮式里程计参数（转向偏置等）与运动构型强耦合，传统模型难以在线准确标定；仅依靠驱动速度的模型会导致转向偏置不可观测。因此需要一个能融合视觉、惯性与冗余轮式约束并支持在线标定的里程计框架，同时明确其可观测性与退化条件。

Method: 1) 直接从四个驱动速度与转向角推导2D里程计模型，利用所有车轮的纵向滚动约束和横向无滑移约束；2) 建立预积分模型与解析雅可比，用于高效滤波与标定；3) 对线性化VIWO系统进行可观测性分析，对比“仅驱动模型”与提出的“冗余模型”；4) 分析退化运动（零偏航角速度、恒定转向、车轮不滚动）并推导固定里程计参考系后剩余十三个轮式内参的激励条件。

Result: 可观测性分析表明：仅驱动模型使所有转向偏置不可观测，而所提冗余模型恢复了其可观测性；分析还识别出四个标准VINS不可观测方向，以及三个与里程计参考系任意放置相关的附加不可观测方向。同时刻画了零偏航角速度、恒定转向、车轮不滚动等退化运动，并给出十三项轮式内参的激励条件。仿真与4WIS4WID机器人实机实验验证了系统性能。

Conclusion: 提出的在线标定VIWO框架适用于4WIS4WID移动机器人，冗余轮式约束模型在保持滤波效率的同时提升了标定可观测性，并为退化运动与内参激励提供了理论指导，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a visual-inertial-wheel odometry (VIWO) framework with online calibration for four-wheel independently steered and driven (4WIS4WID) mobile robots. We derive a 2D odometry model directly from the four driving velocities and steering angles, using both the longitudinal rolling constraints and the lateral no-slip constraints of all wheels. A preintegration model and analytical Jacobians are developed for efficient filtering and calibration. An observability analysis of the linearized VIWO system shows that a drive-only model makes all steering offsets unobservable, whereas the proposed redundant model restores their observability. The analysis also identifies four standard VINS unobservable directions and three additional directions associated with the arbitrary placement of the odometry reference frame. Furthermore, we characterize several degenerate motions, including zero yaw rate, constant steering, and a non-rolling wheel, and derive the corresponding excitation conditions for the thirteen wheel intrinsics that remain after fixing the odometry frame reference. The performance of the proposed system has been demonstrated in both simulation and real-world experiments on a 4WIS4WID mobile robot.

</details>


### [201] [TrafficSignBench: Rule-Centric Closed-Loop Evaluation of Traffic-Sign Compliance in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38463)
*Victoria Smirnova,Viktoriia Zinkovich,Gregorii Bukhtuev,Artem Belyaev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Denis Shepelev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrafficSignBench，一个以交通标志为核心的大规模基准，用于系统、可解释地评估自动驾驶规划器的交通规则遵守情况，并通过显式交通标志约束生成合规轨迹专家用于微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划器通常用驾驶得分、到达率、碰撞率等聚合指标评估，这些指标无法明确衡量交通规则遵守情况。因此规划器可能在基准上取得高分，却仍表现出不安全或违法的行为，限制了实际部署的适用性。

Method: 框架结合基于真实地图的仿真（提供真实道路布局）与面向规则的程序化场景生成（实现可扩展且均衡的覆盖，尤其针对代表性不足的规则）。实现对应 34 种交通标志的交通规则，每种配备自动规则检查器以在闭环执行中检测违规。设计生成 29,000 个多样道路场景与 29 种测试场景类型，并构建 5,800 个测试场景。此外，通过显式交通标志约束将现有规划器转化为规则合规的轨迹专家，以可扩展地生成高质量 oracle 轨迹用于微调。

Result: 构建了 29,000 个道路场景、29 种测试场景类型和 5,800 个测试场景。实验表明，当前自动驾驶规划器尽管在标准评估指标上表现良好，但交通规则遵守情况可能较差。通过显式交通标志约束可将其转化为规则合规的轨迹专家，用于生成微调所需的高质量 oracle 轨迹。

Conclusion: TrafficSignBench 弥补了现有评估在交通规则合规性测量上的空白，实现了对规则特定规划器行为的可控评估，并可借助规则合规轨迹专家为提升规划器合规性提供可扩展的数据生成方案。

Abstract: Autonomous driving planners are typically evaluated using aggregate metrics such as driving score, destination rate, and collision rate, which do not explicitly measure compliance with traffic rules. As a result, planners can achieve high benchmark scores while still exhibiting unsafe or illegal behaviors, limiting their applicability to real-world deployment. To address this gap, we introduce TrafficSignBench, a large-scale, traffic sign-centric benchmark for systematic and interpretable evaluation of traffic-rule compliance in autonomous driving. Our framework combines real-map-based simulation for realistic road layouts with rule-targeted procedural scenario generation for scalable and balanced coverage of underrepresented rules. We implement traffic rules corresponding to 34 traffic signs, each equipped with an automatic rule checker for detecting violations during closed-loop execution. This design yields 29,000 diverse road scenes and 29 distinct testing scenario types, enabling controlled evaluation of rule-specific planner behavior. We construct 5,800 testing scenes and demonstrate that current autonomous driving planners can exhibit poor traffic-rule compliance despite strong performance on standard evaluation metrics. To address this limitation, we transform existing planners into rule-compliant trajectory experts via explicit traffic-sign constraints, enabling scalable generation of high-quality oracle trajectories for fine-tuning.

</details>


### [202] [L1-MPPI: L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral for Agile UAV Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38467)
*Lukáš Kotek,Ondřej Procházka,Vojtěch Vonásek,Martin Saska,Robert Pěnička*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 L1-MPPI，将 L1 自适应控制与模型预测路径积分（MPPI）级联，用于提升高速无人机轨迹跟踪精度，并在仿真与实飞中验证其在未知负载下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向无人机控制的 MPPI 方法通常未显式建模气动效应、变负载，且忽略底层电机控制器动态，导致在模型不确定性与外部扰动（如额外负载、气动阻力建模偏差）下跟踪精度下降。

Method: 将 L1 自适应控制与 MPPI 级联；在 MPPI 所用动态模型中纳入底层飞行控制器与电机动态，并引入反映底层控制器方式的迭代混合（iterative mixing）方案；借助 L1 增广应对模型不确定性与外部扰动。对比基线为普通 MPPI 及以在线质量估计替代 L1 增广的 MPPI。

Result: 在 35% 质量增加的飞行中，相比普通 MPPI 将 RMSE 降低 58.61%；相比使用在线质量估计的 MPPI 降低 38.59%。实飞中速度达 13.50 m/s，加速度达 2.5 g，仿真与实飞均表现出更好的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: L1-MPPI 通过级联 L1 自适应并细化动态模型（含底层控制器与电机动态、迭代混合），在未知负载与模型不确定性下显著提升高速无人机轨迹跟踪精度，具备仿真与实飞的有效性。

Abstract: This work proposes the L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral (L1-MPPI). It cascades L1 adaptive control with the Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) to improve tracking of high-speed UAV trajectories. Thanks to the L1augmentation, the tracking remains accurate even under model uncertainties and external disturbances, such as an additional payload or a mismatch in the modeled aerodynamic drag. In contrast to existing MPPI approaches for UAV control that do not explicitly model aerodynamic effects, varying payloads, and typically neglect the dynamics of low-level motor controllers, our L1-MPPI approach enhances the dynamic model used in the MPPI by incorporating the low-level flight controller and motor dynamics, as well as an iterative mixing scheme that reflects the approach of the low-level controller. The proposed method demonstrates improved tracking performance in both simulation and the real world, even when the UAV is subjected to an unknown payload. In flight with 35% mass increase, our approach lowers the RMSE by 58.61% with respect to plain MPPI. Compared to the same MPPI using an online mass estimator in place of the L1 augmentation, the RMSE is lower by 38.59%. During the real-world experiments the UAV reaches speeds up to 13.50 m/s and accelerations up to 2.5 g.

</details>


### [203] [Diffusion-2BC: Hybrid Diffusion and Regression Training for Offline Behavior Cloning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38472)
*Bruno Maciel Machado,Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Diffusion-2BC，将扩散去噪目标与辅助确定性行为克隆损失结合于共享视觉编码器，提升自动驾驶扩散策略的闭环可靠性并保留多模态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆是自动驾驶策略的离线学习路径，但均方误差回归难以匹配"同一观测对应多个有效动作"的演示数据；扩散策略虽能建模条件多模态动作分布，但在有限数据下视觉特征与控制联合学习时闭环性能不稳定。

Method: Diffusion-2BC 在共享视觉编码器上联合优化扩散去噪目标与辅助确定性行为克隆损失；辅助分支仅在训练阶段使用，推理阶段仍保持基于扩散的生成方式。

Result: 在 Claw 任务中，平均掩码距离误差相比扩散行为克隆基线降低约 10%，相比标准确定性行为克隆降低约 85%；在无路线 CARLA 中，Town01 与 Town02 下均比两个基线的终止前行驶距离显著更远；定性 rollout 显示出不同的路线选择，体现多模态行为。

Conclusion: 辅助回归信号能够提升扩散行为克隆的闭环可靠性，同时在受控基准中保留多模态预测能力。

Abstract: Behavior cloning provides an offline route to autonomous-driving policy learning, but mean-squared-error regression is poorly matched to demonstrations in which one observation admits several valid actions. Diffusion policies can represent conditional multimodal action distributions, yet their closed-loop performance may be unstable when visual features and control are learned from limited data. This paper presents Diffusion-2BC, which combines a diffusion denoising objective with an auxiliary deterministic behavior-cloning loss over a shared visual encoder. The auxiliary branch is used only during training; inference remains diffusion-based. The proposed method is evaluated in the controlled Claw environment and in bird's-eye-view CARLA navigation, including route-conditioned driving, route-free navigation through multiple intersections, and cross-map evaluation from Town01 to Town02. In the Claw task, Diffusion-2BC reduced the mean mask-distance error by approximately 10% relative to a diffusion-based behavior-cloning baseline and by 85% relative to standard deterministic behavior cloning. In route-free CARLA, Diffusion-2BC traveled substantially farther before termination under the evaluation protocol than both baselines in Town01 and Town02. Additional qualitative rollouts revealed distinct route choices, showing the multimodal behavior of the proposed diffusion-based agent. The results indicate that an auxiliary regression signal can improve the closed-loop reliability of diffusion behavior cloning while preserving multimodal prediction in the controlled benchmark.

</details>


### [204] [CADeT: Causal-Aware Deformation Transmission for Indirect Robotic Manipulation of Soft Tissue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38483)
*Junlei Hu,Dominic Jones,Pietro Valdastri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CADeT框架，将结构因果模型与主动感知结合，实现在机器人辅助微创手术中通过间接操作控制深层可变形解剖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助微创手术中，机器人无法直接触及深层可变形解剖结构，中间组织会空间性地过滤形变传递。被动观测存在歧义，因为解耦模式和阻挡模式可能产生相似的形变响应，因此需要新的方法来区分这些模式并实现间接形变控制。

Method: 提出CADeT因果感知形变传递框架，将结构因果模型（SCM）与主动感知相结合，推断潜在传递模式并在线估计状态依赖的粘附雅可比矩阵。正常操作时控制动作更新模式信念；当歧义持续时，选择额外的探测动作以提高模式可区分性。将模式信念与学习的雅可比矩阵融入信念感知模型预测控制器，实现间接目标形状控制。

Result: 在仿真和da Vinci研究套件（dVRK）上进行验证，使用体模和离体猪组织，结果显示模式识别准确率更高、形状误差收敛更快，优于所评估的无模型和基于模型的基线方法。

Conclusion: 主动感知能够在所评估的条件下改善模式识别和间接形变控制，验证了CADeT框架的有效性。

Abstract: Indirect manipulation of deep-seated deformable anatomy inaccessible to the robot is challenging in robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS) because intervening tissues spatially filter deformation transmission. Passive observations can be ambiguous because the Decoupled and Blocked modes may produce similar motion responses. We propose CADeT, a causal-aware deformation transmission framework that integrates structural causal model (SCM) with active sensing to infer a latent transmission mode and estimate a state-dependent adhesion Jacobian online. During normal manipulation, control actions update the mode belief; when ambiguity persists, an additional probing action is selected to improve mode distinguishability. The mode belief and learned Jacobian are incorporated into a belief-aware model predictive controller for indirect target-shape control. Validation in simulation and on the da Vinci research kit (dVRK), using phantom and ex vivo porcine tissues, shows higher mode-identification accuracy and faster shape-error convergence than the evaluated model-free and model-based baselines. These results show that active sensing improves mode identification and indirect deformation control under the evaluated conditions.

</details>


### [205] [What to Attend, What to Keep: Skill-Conditioned Visuotactile Representation with Progress-Guided Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38494)
*Amir-Hossein Shahidzadeh,Seungjae Lee,Eadom Dessalene,Shanthosh Raaj Mohanram Mageswari,Soroush Etemad,Furong Huang,Cornelia Fermüller,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种技能条件化的多模态表征：以技能为查询条件，对视觉与触觉的短时观测token进行融合，并稀疏地关注保存过去K个技能终端观测的事件记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人多模态操作策略普遍采用固定的时间上下文与融合策略，但视觉与触觉在不同技能、不同阶段中的贡献是动态变化的（视觉引导接近，触觉随时间演化决定抓取、对齐与接触）。因此需要研究在"基本技能"层面视触觉应如何结合，即每个技能需要各传感器提供什么。

Method: 设计技能条件化表征：以当前查询的技能作为条件，指导对模态特异短时观测token的融合，同时注意力机制访问一个稀疏事件记忆——该记忆保留最近K个已执行技能的终端观测。

Result: 在三个富接触任务上以技能进度估计为评测：滑移检测延迟相比微调的SOTA进度模型降低87%；扭转完成延迟相比纯视觉消融降低67.5%；盲搜索任务在稀疏事件记忆下进度误差降低92%。提升恰好集中于完成由接触或任务历史定义之处。

Conclusion: 结果表明，观测的形成（observation formation）而不只是策略架构，是多模态表征中的核心挑战。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation integrates vision, touch, and language, whose importance shifts across stages: vision guides reaching, while touch, through its evolution over time, decides grasping, alignment, and contact. Yet existing multi-modal manipulation policies typically use fixed temporal contexts and fusion strategies, despite shifts in what each modality contributes across different skills. We study how vision and touch should be combined at the level of primitive skills, asking what each skill needs from each sensor, and propose a skill-conditioned representation in which the queried skill conditions fusion over modality-specific short-term observation tokens while attending to a sparse event memory that retains terminal observations from the last $K$ executed skills. Evaluated by skill progress estimation on three contact-rich tasks, it reduces slip-detection delay by 87% against fine-tuned SOTA progress models, twist-completion delay by 67.5% against a vision-only ablation, and progress error on a blind search task by 92% through sparse event memory. Gains concentrate exactly where completion is defined by contact or task history. More broadly, our results suggest that observation formation not only policy architecture is a central challenge in multi-modal representation. Project Website: http://what-to-attend-what-to-keep.github.io/

</details>


### [206] [Onboard Vision and MPC Navigation for Underwater Robots: An Open BlueROV2 Platform for Multi-Robot Experiments & Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38511)
*Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Wiktor Kowalczyk,Cezary Banaszek,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源的BlueROV2平台，集成板载视觉与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），用于受限环境下的自主导航与对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人需要在受限环境中具备鲁棒的感知、估计与控制能力，本文旨在提供可复现的开源平台以支持相关开发与研究。

Method: 平台集成NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX与Intel RealSense D435i立体相机于模块化耐压舱中；采用水下标定的立体深度与实时目标检测获取邻近BlueROV2的相对位置；基于四元数的估计器融合外部位姿与惯性测量；基于六自由度非线性模型的NMPC控制器跟踪导航与对接轨迹；并提供开源PX4 SITL与Gazebo仿真环境、多机器人仿真工具及低成本物理对接站。

Result: 实验评估了水下感知、板载计算性能、状态估计、轨迹跟踪与自主对接。

Conclusion: 该开源平台有助于促进可复现的自主水下导航与对接研究与开发。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots require robust perception, estimation and control to operate in confined environments. This paper presents an open-source BlueROV2 platform combining onboard vision with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) for autonomous navigation and docking. The platform integrates an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX and an Intel RealSense D435i stereo camera in a modular pressure housing. Underwater-calibrated stereo depth and realtime object detection provide relative position measurements of nearby BlueROV2 vehicles in the camera and body frames. A quaternion-based estimator fuses external pose and inertial measurements, while an NMPC controller based on a nonlinear six-degree-of-freedom model tracks planned navigation and docking trajectories. To support reproducible development, we also provide open-source physics-based PX4 SITL and Gazebo environments, multi-robot simulation tools and a lowcost physical docking station. Experiments evaluate underwater perception, onboard computational performance, state estimation, trajectory tracking and autonomous docking.

</details>


### [207] [Behavioral Persistence and Incomplete Functional Transfer of Co-evolved Communication in Evolutionary Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38527)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究将2D仿真中协同演化出的通信协议直接迁移到3D物理机器人环境（不重新训练权重），发现迁移仅部分成功且存在不对称性，说明成功迁移不仅依赖信号过程本身，还依赖协议演化时的生态与导航条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在仿真中协同演化出的涌现通信协议能否不经过再训练直接迁移到真实物理机器人上，从而探讨涌现通信在"仿真到现实"迁移中的可行性与瓶颈。

Method: 使用两台e-puck型机器人，由带残差连接的GRU网络控制，在带社交信号的觅食任务中进行评估。传感与运动转换层做了三处修正以保证物理运行的稳定性，其中包括依据训练残差权重中可测量的不对称性来校准"饥饿项"。另增设一个在社交通道中显式加入方向信息的实验。

Result: 即使经过修正，迁移仍是部分且不对称的：在30个测试种子中，一个智能体仅有1次到达食物源，另一个则从未到达（任务成功需两个智能体都到达食物区）。加入显式方向信息后，接收方智能体的轨迹出现可观察变化，若干具体情形有所改善，但仍不足以让第二个智能体到达食物源。

Conclusion: 迁移的局限不能仅由信号转换解释，还受制于新物理约束下的导航能力；涌现通信的成功迁移可能不仅取决于保留信号过程本身，还取决于保留协议演化时所处的生态与导航条件。

Abstract: This work evaluates the direct transfer of a co-evolved communication protocol from a 2D simulation to a 3D physical environment, without retraining the network weights. Two e-puck-type robots, controlled by a GRU network with residual connection, were evaluated in a food-seeking task with social signaling. The sensory and motor translation layer required three corrections for stable physical operation, including the calibration of a hunger term based on a measurable asymmetry in the trained residual weights. Even with these corrections, the transfer was partial and asymmetric: one agent reached the food source in one of thirty tested seeds, while the other did not reach it in any. Task success was measured by both agents reaching the food area. An additional experiment incorporating explicit directional information in the social channel produced observable changes in the trajectory of the receiving agent and improvements in several specific cases. However, these improvements were not enough to allow the second agent to reach the food source, suggesting that the limitation may not be explained solely by signal translation, but also by the ability to navigate under the new physical constraints. The results suggest that successful transfer of emergent communication may depend not only on preserving the signaling process itself, but also on preserving the ecological and navigational conditions under which the protocol evolved.

</details>


### [208] [Systematically Exploring the Capabilities of GPT-6 Astra as Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38537)
*Galbot Team,Xuchuan Chen,Xiaoqian Cheng,Yu Deng,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Xiangjun Gao,Haozhe Jia,Zekai Li,Zhoujian Li,Yunrui Lian,Sikai Liang,Chenghuai Lin,Dairu Liu,Jiahang Liu,Qingtao Liu,Yuxuan Ma,Zekun Qi,Jiayi Su,He Wang,Ruochen Xu,Tianyu Xu,Xudong Xu,Zhe Xu,Mi Yan,Siming Yan,Li Yi,Ruixi Yu,Jinlu Zhang,Yintianrun Zhang,Zhikai Zhang,Zhizheng Zhang,Yixin Zheng,Weiyi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文全面评估GPT-6 Astra作为通用具身策略的能力，覆盖夹爪操作、灵巧操作、移动操作、导航、运动与人形全身操作六大领域，发现其能提供有用的高层任务决策，但在可靠的物理控制上仍存在明显差距，且推理延迟严重制约实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GPT-6 Astra能否超越高层规划角色，直接生成数值化机器人动作，成为通用具身策略，并系统评估其能力边界与局限。

Method: 在六大领域开展综合评测，涵盖直接控制、与学习策略协作（如混合控制结合π0.5）以及反馈驱动的自适应三类范式，并统计推理延迟与token消耗。
